We describe the single mid-run human intervention that pulled the community out of a monoculture, what the trace does and does not establish, and the controlled comparison that would settle whether shared research state improves discovery per unit of compute.

</details>


### [233] [iMINDBench: iEEG Multi-Institution Neural Decoding Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18104)
*Geeling Chau,Saba Hashemi,Yonghyeon Gwon,Eshani Patel,Jan DeWitt,Christopher Wang,Andrii Zahorodnii,Sabera J Talukder,Danny Dongyeop Han,Chun Kee Chung,Maryam M Shanechi,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: iMINDBench 是一个跨机构的颅内脑电图（iEEG）神经解码基准测试，包含15个自然电影观看任务，旨在统一评估模型在不同数据集和预处理流程下的表现。该基准定义了标准化的预处理流程和固定评估划分，以支持可比性更强的模型比较。实验表明，预训练模型在各自预处理流程中优于基线，但强谱域基线仍具竞争力；增加25倍额外数据仅带来小幅度或任务依赖性的提升，凸显了改进预处理基线及跨受试者、跨机构数据利用效率的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前iEEG解码研究受限于任务或机构特定的数据集，缺乏通用性验证；同时预处理方法对性能影响显著，导致难以区分模型改进与预处理带来的提升。因此需要一个标准化的基准来推动可靠、可复现的模型评估与比较。

Method: 构建iMINDBench基准，整合三个自然电影观看任务的数据集，涵盖15个解码任务；设定标准化预处理流程和固定评估划分；在多个预处理路径下评估多种预训练模型，并进行数据规模扩展实验。

Result: 预训练模型在各自预处理路径下普遍优于基线；强谱域基线在跨机构数据上仍具竞争力；增加25倍外部数据仅带来微弱或任务依赖性的性能提升，表明现有模型对跨受试者/机构数据的利用效率不足。

Conclusion: 当前iEEG解码模型需超越强预处理基线，发展更高效的数据泛化能力，尤其在跨个体和跨机构场景下，亟需新方法提升数据利用率和模型通用性。

Abstract: Intracranial electroencephalography (iEEG) is widely used to record electrical activity directly from electrodes inside the human brain, making it an attractive modality for neural decoding. However, progress in iEEG decoding, especially toward general-purpose foundation models, remains difficult to measure reliably: datasets are task- or institution-specific, limiting evidence of generalization across tasks and recording environments, and preprocessing choices can strongly influence performance, making model improvements difficult to distinguish from preprocessing gains. Thus, we introduce iMINDBench, an iEEG Multi-Institution Neural Decoding Benchmark that evaluates models on a shared suite of fifteen decoding tasks across three naturalistic movie-watching datasets. The benchmark additionally defines standardized preprocessing tracks and fixed evaluation splits to support consistent model comparisons. Using iMINDBench, we find that the evaluated pretrained systems generally outperform baselines within their respective preprocessing tracks, while strong spectral baselines remain competitive across institutional datasets. In our scaling study, adding up to 25 times more supervised data from other subjects or institutions yields only small or task-dependent gains over within-session training. Together, these findings highlight the need for iEEG models that improve on strong preprocessing baselines and make more effective use of data across subjects and institutions. Project website: https://imindbench.github.io/

</details>


### [234] [FoundAna: A GNN-assisted Foundation Model for Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18107)
*Suprim Nakarmi,Chahana Dahal,Yue Zhao,Junggab Son,Zuobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FoundAna 是首个专为图异常检测设计的基于 GNN 和 Transformer 的基础模型框架，旨在实现跨图、可迁移的异常检测。它通过结合 GNN 与增强位置编码的 Transformer，捕捉局部和全局结构信息，并利用重建误差作为异常评分，在九个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图异常检测方法多采用单一模型对应单一数据集的范式，难以在不同任务、标签稀缺和领域差异下迁移，限制了其在真实场景中的应用。因此需要一种通用性强、可跨图迁移的模型框架。

Method: FoundAna 结合了专门用于异常检测的 GNN 组件与带有四种互补位置编码的标准化 Transformer 编码器，通过节点表示的重构误差（来自属性和邻接关系解码器）生成异常分数。

Result: 在金融、社交和引文网络等领域的九个基准数据集上，FoundAna 均显著优于当前最先进方法，展现出优异的泛化能力和跨图适应性。

Conclusion: FoundAna 成功构建了首个面向图异常检测的基础模型框架，实现了通用性和高性能的统一，为未来跨域图异常检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Graph anomaly detection aims to identify graph structures (e.g., nodes, edges, or subgraphs) that deviate significantly from expected patterns, which supports critical applications in fraud detection, spam identification, network intrusion, etc. Despite the growing methods in the field, existing approaches follow a one-model-per-dataset paradigm, limiting their transferability across diverse real-world scenarios due to task heterogeneity, label scarcity, and domain variability. In this work, we introduce FoundAna, a GNN-assisted Foundation Model for Graph Anomaly Detection - the first foundation model framework designated for generalizable, cross-graph anomaly detection by combining GNNs and transformers. FoundAna integrates an anomaly detection-specific GNN component with a standard transformer encoder augmented by four complementary positional encodings, which enable the model to capture both local and global structural information. Specifically, the positional encoding enriched node representations are passed through attribute and adjacency decoders, and the reconstruction errors serve as the anomaly score. Extensive experiments on nine benchmark datasets spanning financial, social, and citation network domains demonstrate that FoundAna consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. The code implementation and Supplementary materials are here: https://github.com/FoundAna331/FoundAna.

</details>


### [235] [Learning Fractional-Order Dynamics from a Single Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18127)
*Xiaole Zhang,Ziyi Zhang,Zehao Zhao,Stephen Tu,Guannan Qu,Yorie Nakahira,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了从单个轨迹中识别离散时间分数阶线性时不变系统，提出了一种名为FO-GS的两阶段估计器，利用分数差分算子的对角结构实现行解耦。在稳定性假设下，建立了非渐近、高概率的误差界，估计误差均以$\mathcal{O}(t^{-1/2})$收敛，并通过实验验证其在恢复分数阶和系统动态方面的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现实世界过程表现出长程依赖性，当前状态依赖于过去状态的缓慢衰减痕迹，而非仅依赖最近状态。传统马尔可夫系统难以捕捉此类非马尔可夫动力学，因此需要新的方法来识别具有分数阶特性的系统。

Method: 提出分数阶普通最小二乘网格搜索（FO-GS）算法，通过利用格林沃尔德-莱特尼克夫差分算子的对角结构，将识别问题分解为逐行处理，从而实现计算上的解耦与简化。

Result: 在稳定性假设下，理论证明了对分数阶和系统矩阵的估计误差均以$\mathcal{O}(t^{-1/2})$速率收敛，且具有高概率非渐近界；实验表明，该方法在恢复分数阶和系统动态方面优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: FO-GS是一种有效且高效的分数阶系统识别方法，适用于具有长程依赖性的非马尔可夫过程，为复杂动态系统的建模与分析提供了理论支持与实践工具。

Abstract: Many real-world processes exhibit long-range dependence, where the current state depends on a slowly decaying trace of past states rather than on the most recent state alone. This paper studies system identification for discrete-time fractional-order linear time-invariant systems from a single observed trajectory of length $t$, a setting that captures such non-Markovian dynamics through the Grünwald--Letnikov difference operator. Unlike Markovian systems, fractional-order systems couple estimation across the entire history, making both statistical analysis and practical identification more challenging. We propose \emph{Fractional-Order Ordinary-Least-Squares Grid-Search (FO-GS)}, a simple two-stage estimator that exploits the diagonal structure of the fractional-difference operator to decouple the identification problem row-wise. Under the stability assumption, we establish high-probability, non-asymptotic error bounds for estimating both the fractional order and the system matrix in the heterogeneous setting, with both estimation errors scaling as \(\mathcal{O}(t^{-1/2})\). Through experiments, we show that \emph{FO-GS} outperforms existing baselines in recovering both the fractional order and the underlying system dynamics.

</details>


### [236] [Colla-Q: Toward Collaborative Experts in MoE Quantization via Minimax Precision Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18131)
*Eunju Shin,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于激活熵的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）量化方法Colla-Q，通过激活熵指导比特位分配，实现专家间性能均衡，提升量化后MoE模型的整体性能并降低对校准数据集的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化虽能降低内存和计算开销，但在MoE模型中易导致性能显著下降，尤其因各专家参数量小且对低比特表示敏感，个别专家性能衰退会严重影响整体模型表现。因此需一种能保持专家间协同、避免性能失衡的量化方法。

Method: 提出基于激活熵的比特位分配算法，动态为不同专家分配合适比特宽度，使各专家在量化后仍能协同工作，实现性能均衡。

Result: Colla-Q有效提升了量化MoE模型的整体性能，增强了模型在不同校准数据集上的泛化能力与稳定性，减少了对特定校准数据的依赖。

Conclusion: 所提出的Colla-Q量化方法通过激活熵引导比特分配，实现了MoE模型在量化后的性能平衡与稳定，具备良好的泛化能力与实用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) quantization method based on activation entropy. Although quantization reduces memory and computational costs, it can substantially degrade performance. In particular, performance decline is pronounced in quantized MoE models, where individual experts have a small number of parameters that are sensitive to low-bit representation. Considering that MoE operates as an ensemble model with collaborative contributions from routed experts, a significant performance decline of a particular expert due to quantization can harm model performance. Therefore, we propose Colla-Q, a bit-allocation framework to maintain balanced performance across experts through an activation-entropy-based bit-width allocation algorithm. This approach encourages each expert to operate collaboratively in the quantized model, thereby 1) improving the overall MoE performance and 2) reducing the dependence on the calibration dataset. Since uniformly adjusting each expert's performance facilitates robustness and stability of the MoE model, the proposed MoE quantization method can generalize more consistently across different calibration datasets. Our code is available at: https://github.com/mmai-laboratory/Colla_Q

</details>


### [237] [Rethinking How We Evaluate Methodological Progress in Health AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18134)
*Florent Pollet,Matthew McDermott*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在统一评估框架下重实现12个历史与近期的AI算法，评估其在MIMIC-IV和NWICU两个临床数据集上的表现，研究了算法比较在不同任务类型（专家定义的临床任务与随机生成的任务）和数据集之间的可转移性。结果发现，算法间的相对表现具有较强跨设置一致性，包括从随机任务到临床有意义任务的迁移；同时，临床任务表现出更高的任务-方法交互性，提示任务特性可能解释特定建模选择的优势。此外，近年算法并未持续优于早期方法，梯度提升树在结合现代稀疏宽表示时仍具竞争力。研究表明，方法学进步可能不需要过多任务工程，但需关注任务与方法间结构化异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI在电子健康记录（EHR）中的进展受限于可复现性难题以及缺乏临床意义明确的评估任务。为理解这些障碍，本文旨在通过系统性重实现与对比分析，探究算法性能比较在不同任务和数据集间的可转移性及其背后的方法学启示。

Method: 在统一评估框架中重实现12个代表性算法，使用两个临床数据集（MIMIC-IV和NWICU），评估两类任务：由专家定义的临床有意义任务，以及基于随机事件代码和预测时间点生成的随机任务。通过配对比较算法性能，分析其在不同任务家族与数据集间的可转移性，并考察任务-方法交互效应。

Result: 1. 算法间的相对性能在不同任务类型和数据集间表现出强可转移性，即使从随机任务推广至临床任务也成立；2. 临床任务显示出更高程度的任务-方法交互，表明任务特性可能影响模型表现优势；3. 近年开发的新算法并未始终优于早期方法，梯度提升树在现代表示下依然表现优异。

Conclusion: 尽管存在任务异质性，算法性能比较仍具有较强的跨任务和跨数据集稳定性，表明方法学知识的积累可能不依赖过度任务工程。然而，任务特性仍能揭示模型选择的优势条件，提示未来研究应更关注任务与方法之间的结构性关系。

Abstract: Methodological progress in artificial intelligence (AI) for electronic health records (EHRs) depends on our ability to determine which algorithms work better, and under which conditions. However, such progress is thought to be hindered by difficulties in reproducibility and in defining clinically meaningful evaluation tasks. We empirically study these barriers by re-implementing 12 historical and recent algorithms within a shared evaluation framework and evaluating them on two clinical datasets, MIMIC-IV and NWICU. We compare two complementary task families: expert-authored clinically meaningful tasks and generated tasks defined from randomly sampled event codes and prediction horizons. We ask whether relative algorithms comparisons transfer across task families and datasets, whether residual task heterogeneity contains useful methodological structure, and what a controlled comparison reveals about progress over the last decade. We find that aggregate pairwise comparisons transfer strongly across evaluation settings, including from randomly generated tasks to clinically meaningful tasks and across datasets. At the same time, clinically meaningful tasks exhibit greater task-method interaction, providing preliminary evidence that task properties can help explain when particular modeling choices are advantageous. Finally, newer algorithms do not consistently outperform earlier approaches: gradient-boosted trees remain highly competitive when paired with a modern, wide and sparse representation of the EHR. Together, these results suggest that useful methodological knowledge may require less task engineering than commonly assumed, while highlighting the importance of understanding the structured heterogeneity that remains across tasks and methods.

</details>


### [238] [Reaching Every Position Without Searching: Rotating Sparse Wiring on the Hypercube as a Substitute for Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18145)
*Yoshiaki Takashita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种固定、稀疏且逐层旋转的连接机制，替代传统的注意力机制。通过将序列位置视为高维超立方体的顶点，并在每层沿特定维度连接邻居，信息可在 log₂n 层内传播至所有位置，仅需 2n 条链接（相比全连接的 n²）。在合成任务中，该方法以 1/32 的链接数达到与全连接相当的效果；在 enwik8 字符级语言建模任务中，混合模型（16 层中保留2层注意力）比同等宽度的全注意力模型降低 0.06 bit/char 的损失，同时减少 1/7 链接、42% 参数量、节省 2.4 倍运行时间；纯旋转方案表现相当。在日英混合代码语料上差距更大（0.16），学习率窗口也宽 4-8 倍。文中还指出部分无效尝试（如学习坐标、动态变体）及关键实验规范（冻结语料、全覆盖评估、多种子验证）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统注意力机制计算开销大，尤其在长序列上。本文旨在探索是否可通过固定、稀疏且结构化的连接模式实现类似甚至超越注意力的效果，从而大幅降低计算成本并提升训练效率。

Method: 将序列位置映射为 log₂n 维超立方体的顶点，每层按维度 (ℓ mod log₂n) 连接相邻节点，形成旋转式稀疏连接。结合少量全注意力层构成混合架构，或完全采用旋转连接。使用固定语料、多种子验证和全覆盖评估进行训练与测试。

Result: 在合成任务中，旋转连接以 1/32 的连接数达到全连接效果；在 enwik8 上，混合模型比全注意力模型低 0.06 bit/char 损失，链接减少 1/7，参数降 42%，耗时降 2.4 倍；纯旋转方案性能相当；在日英混合语料上优势更明显（0.16），学习率窗口扩大 4-8 倍。无效尝试包括学习坐标和饱和核的动态变体。

Conclusion: 固定、稀疏且逐层旋转的连接模式可有效替代注意力机制，在保持高性能的同时显著降低计算资源消耗。关键在于“每个维度都被访问”，而非连接顺序。该设计在多个任务中表现优异，且具备更强的训练鲁棒性与效率。

Abstract: Attention pays, at every layer and for every input, the cost of searching for whom to connect. We ask how far one can get with wiring that is fixed, sparse, and simply rotated from layer to layer. Treating the $n$ positions of a sequence as the vertices of a $\log_2 n$-dimensional hypercube and connecting each position, at layer $\ell$, to its neighbour along dimension $\ell \bmod \log_2 n$, information from every position reaches every other in $\log_2 n$ layers with $2n$ links per layer instead of $n^2$. On a synthetic task that is unsolvable unless all positions are reached, this rotation matches all-to-all wiring at $1/32$ of the links, while the same sparse pattern held fixed across layers fails; what matters is that every dimension is touched, not the order. On character-level language modelling of a public corpus (the first $12$M characters of enwik8), a hybrid that keeps two attention layers among sixteen sparse ones reaches $0.06$ bits-per-character lower held-out loss than a fully attentive model of the same width at the same step budget (three seeds each, no overlap), with $1/7$ of the links, $42\%$ fewer parameters, and $2.4\times$ less wall-clock time; the purely rotated schedule is level with the hybrid. The same ordering holds on a second corpus of mixed Japanese, English and code, where the gap widens to $0.16$. The usable learning-rate window is four to eight times wider than attention's on both. We also report what did not work - learned coordinates, and a "dynamics" variant whose apparent gains turned out to be an artefact of a saturated kernel - and the measurement discipline (frozen corpus, full-coverage evaluation, seed spread as the bar for ranking) that we found necessary to say anything at all at this scale.

</details>


### [239] [LIGE-GR: A Smooth Leap from Ranking to Generative Recommendation in the LLM Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18148)
*Venkat Srinivas,Chenzhang He,Sam Woodmansee,Shawn Lian,Wenjie Hu,Renjie Jiang,Ziheng Huang,Xinyuan Zhang,Zhihao Zheng,Zhuoran Yu,Rui Li,Lei Yuan,Ziwei Li,Jimmy Jia,Mert Terzihan,Ekrem Kocaguneli,Yiming Liao,Zhichen Zhao,Yue Yin,Yue Weng,Wanlin Ma,Xufeng Cai,Weimiao Wu,Yezhou Huang,Du Zhang,Yukun Ding,Aaron Johnston,Yueming Wang,Zhaojie Gong,Yuting Zhang,Serena Li,Adithya Ganesh,Boying Liu,Haichuan Yang,Xialu Li,Matt Ma,Qunshu Zhang,John Joshua Miller,Praveen Rathinavelu,Cheng Huang,Aadhar Sachdeva,Josh Karns,Andres Aaron Gutierrez,Neil Agarwal,Gustas Pladis,Vladimir Batygin,Gopal Ray,Aditya Priyadarshi,Shantanu Patil,Zhe Wang,Penny Pan,Yiping Han,Arun Singh,Guangdeng Liao,Bi Xue,Xinyao Hu,Yang Song,Yisong Song,Meihong Wang,Haotian Wu,Deepak Agarwal,Ji Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LIGE-GR 是一种从传统点级推荐系统升级到列表级生成推荐范式的框架，通过将现有推荐系统泛化为列表级生成系统，在不破坏现有模型、价值函数和部署基础设施的前提下，实现列表级优化。在 Instagram Reels 和 Facebook Video 上的实验表明，该方法分别提升了 1.14% 和 0.72% 的用户停留时间，且推理资源开销较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）的成功为下一代推荐系统提供了重要启示，但如何将 LLM 的序列生成与优化机制精准融入成熟的工业推荐系统仍是一个开放问题。主要挑战在于：如何将序列级生成与优化引入推荐系统，以及如何在不破坏现有成熟系统的基础上进行升级。

Method: 提出 LIGE-GR 框架，将传统的点级推荐系统泛化为列表级生成系统，实现列表级优化，同时保持与现有模型、价值函数和部署基础设施的兼容性。

Result: 在 Instagram Reels 和 Facebook Video 的短视频推荐场景中，LIGE-GR 分别带来 1.14% 和 0.72% 的用户停留时间提升，且仅需少量额外推理资源。

Conclusion: LIGE-GR 成功实现了从点级推荐到列表级生成推荐的平滑升级，为成熟工业推荐系统吸收 LLM 优势提供了一种可行路径，兼具有效性与工程可行性。

Abstract: The remarkable success of large language models (LLMs) has provided important inspiration for the next generation of recommender systems. Structurally, recommendation and language generation share a similarity: both aim to produce an ordered sequence that optimizes the user's experience. However, how to precisely absorb the essence of the LLM paradigm into mature industrial recommender systems remains an open problem.
  There are two challenges. First, it is unclear how to incorporate sequence-level generation and optimization from the LLM paradigm into recommendation. Second, real-world recommender systems are mature systems that have been iteratively customized for years around specific products, business constraints, serving infrastructure, and organizational ownership. Replacing such systems wholesale is often technically risky and organizationally disruptive.
  In this paper, we propose LIGE-GR, a listwise generation and evaluation recommendation framework that upgrades from a traditional ranking system based on itemwise recommendation toward a generative recommendation paradigm. Instead of rebuilding the entire recommendation stack from scratch, LIGE-GR generalizes the existing pointwise recommendation system into a listwise generation system. This allows mature recommender systems to benefit from listwise optimization while preserving compatibility with existing models, value functions, and serving infrastructure.
  We validate LIGE-GR in short-video recommendation on Instagram Reels and Facebook Video. On these recommendation surfaces, LIGE-GR improves time spent by 1.14 percent on Instagram Reels and 0.72 percent on Facebook Video, while requiring only modest additional inference resources.

</details>


### [240] [MoRE: Mixture of Reused Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18176)
*Eric S. Qiu,Utku Umur Acikalin,Justin Lovelace,Christian Belardi,Arjun B. Mulchandani,Carla P. Gomes,Kilian Q. Weinberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mixture of Reused Experts (MoRE) combines the benefits of MoE and Recurrent Transformers by sharing expert pools across adjacent layers, enabling higher model capacity without increasing parameters. It uses lightweight depth embeddings to differentiate expert usage across layers, achieving better language modeling performance than standard MoEs and weight-sharing models at similar compute and parameter budgets.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To address the high memory footprint of MoE architectures and the limited capacity of weight-sharing models like Recurrent Transformers, this paper proposes a hybrid approach that maintains parameter efficiency while improving model performance.

Method: MoRE introduces shared expert pools across groups of adjacent layers, with each layer having its own router. Lightweight learnable depth embeddings are added to condition inputs before routing, allowing shared experts to adapt to different layers.

Result: Experiments on three model scales (114M-1.15B parameters) show MoRE achieves lower perplexity and superior downstream performance compared to standard MoEs and state-of-the-art weight-sharing architectures, with minimal changes to existing MoE implementations.

Conclusion: MoRE effectively balances model capacity and parameter efficiency by reusing experts across layers while maintaining strong performance, offering a practical improvement for large-scale language models.

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures decouple model capacity from computational cost, yet incur high memory footprints as parameters grow linearly with the number of experts. Recurrent Transformers achieve parameter efficiency by reusing layer weights, but typically lack the capacity for competitive language modeling. We propose Mixture of Reused Experts (MoRE), a hybrid that shares expert pools across groups of adjacent layers. Each layer retains its own router but selects from a larger shared pool, expanding the diversity of routing combinations without additional parameters. To enable shared experts to distinguish between layers, we introduce lightweight learnable depth embeddings that condition each layer's input before routing. Experiments across three model scales (114M-1.15B parameters) show that MoRE consistently achieves lower perplexity and stronger downstream performance than standard MoEs and state-of-the-art weight-sharing architectures at matched compute and parameter budgets, with only minimal modifications to existing MoE implementations.

</details>


### [241] [Transformation Laws in Neural Representations: Structure, Realisability, and Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18190)
*Yuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经表征如何保持输入变化的结构，通过参考变换在神经特征上的兼容操作来探索可操作的表征内容。提出了一种线性设定，其中变换失败的原因由其对被丢弃信息的需求决定。对于整流器，变换无法实现的两个来源是：源区域已不可恢复的信息，以及用单一算子满足所有访问区域的成本。针对测量谐波载波，存在闭式解：当保留的谐波块在变换作用下不变时，线性实现存在。以颜色为例，发现冻结视觉特征中的色调轨道在前两个谐波中集中了84-88%的能量，且旋转平面在不同形状间共享；这种组织结构主要来自输入和架构，并随训练和深度重塑。测量结构支持预测、从新起点迁移和组合，全局与局部实现表现差异显著。基于测量结果，构建了一个紧凑接口，其旋转动作由结构固定，无需拟合，在未见形状上实现3.4°的零样本中位误差。理论、结构测量与构造共同确立了变换定律作为连接神经表征理解与设计的实体对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经表征如何保持输入变化的结构，从而将表征分析与内部干预相结合，推动神经网络的设计与可解释性。

Method: 通过参考变换在神经特征上的兼容操作分析可操作表征内容，建立线性框架分析变换失败原因，结合色彩实例进行结构测量，利用测量结果构建无须拟合的紧凑接口。

Result: 在未见形状上，所构建的接口实现了3.4°的零样本中位误差；测量结构支持预测、迁移和组合，全局与局部实现方式表现出显著差异。

Conclusion: 变换定律成为连接神经表征理解与设计的实体对象，为神经网络的可解释性和可控设计提供了理论基础与实践路径。

Abstract: How neural representations preserve the structure of input changes connects representation analysis with internal intervention. We study operable representational content through compatible actions of reference transformations on neural features. We characterise when a transformation descends through an encoder, and give a linear setting in which the defect is governed by the transformation's demand for discarded information, measured in the metric the representation induces. On a rectifier the failure to realise a transformation has two distinguishable sources --- what the source region has already made unrecoverable, and what it costs to satisfy every region the transformation visits with one operator --- and for a \textit{measured} harmonic carrier the same question has a closed answer: a linear realisation exists exactly when the retained harmonic blocks are invariant under the action. Using colour as the in-depth instance, we find that hue orbits in frozen visual features concentrate 84--88\% of their energy in the first two harmonics with rotation planes shared across shapes, that this organisation is substantially inherited from input and architecture and is reshaped by training and depth, and that the measured structure supports prediction, transport from new starting states, and composition --- with global and local realisations differing sharply in which they achieve. Guided by the measurements, we construct a compact interface whose rotation action is fixed by the structure and never fitted: it reads hue zero-shot at 3.4$^\circ$ median error on unseen shapes. Theory, structural measurement, and construction together establish transformation laws as a concrete object connecting the understanding of neural representations to their design.

</details>


### [242] [A Lightweight CNN Integrated Compact Convolutional Transformer for Multi-Scale Feature Learning and reducing computational complexity for breast cancer mammography image detection and classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18212)
*Md Taimur Ahad,Ainuddin Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种CNN与紧凑卷积变压器（CCT）集成的新型框架，用于乳腺癌分类。通过在CNN后引入CCT层，将特征转换为紧凑的图像块标记并添加位置嵌入以保留空间结构，从而有效捕捉长距离上下文依赖。模型仅使用250,435个参数，在3个乳腺癌乳腺钼靶数据集上达到99%-100%的准确率，展现出优异的泛化能力。结合可解释人工智能（XAI），增强了临床信任。该方法克服了CNN在深层梯度消失的问题，且比ViT更轻量，适用于资源受限的临床环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CNN在处理医学图像时难以捕捉长距离上下文依赖，而纯Transformer模型参数量大、计算成本高，难以在资源受限的临床环境中应用。因此需要一种兼具高效性与强表达能力的模型来提升乳腺癌检测的准确性与可解释性。

Method: 在CNN提取特征后引入紧凑卷积变压器（CCT）架构，利用CCT分词器将特征图转化为紧凑的图像块标记，并加入位置嵌入以保持空间结构；随后采用Transformer机制建模长距离依赖关系；结合可解释人工智能（XAI）增强决策透明度。

Result: 模型在3个乳腺癌乳腺钼靶数据集上均达到99%-100%的准确率，仅需250,435个参数，表现出卓越的泛化能力和高效性。可解释性分析揭示了模型关注的关键病变区域，提升了临床可信度。

Conclusion: 所提出的CNN-CCT集成框架有效解决了CNN在深层梯度衰减和长距离依赖建模方面的局限，同时保持轻量化优势，适合部署于资源受限的临床辅助诊断系统中，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Over the years, Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) have demonstrated strong capability in cancer detection and classification using medical images. However, CNN-based models often struggle to capture long-range contextual dependencies. In such scenarios, integrating Compact Convolutional Transformer (CCT) architectures after the CCT layer allows CNN-extracted features to reshape into compact patch tokens using a CCT tokenizer, followed by the addition of positional embeddings to preserve spatial structure. Using 5-fold cross-validation, the model was tested on 3 sets of breast cancer mammography. With only 250,435 parameters, the model achieved 99%-100% accuracy across 3 datasets, indicating robust generalization. Explainable AI (XAI) was integrated into the model to explain the breast cancer classification process to enhance clinical trust. The results indicate that the proposed framework is suitable for computer-aided diagnosis systems, particularly in resource-constrained clinical environments. The novelty of the proposed CNN-integrated CCT overcomes the limitation of CNN's gradient degradation in the last layers by integrating convolutional tokenization with transformer-based learning. Lighter than ViT, which is effective in capturing long-range dependencies, the model has also proven efficient in breast cancer classification by capturing long-range dependencies among breast tissue regions.

</details>


### [243] [APGEM: Adaptive Policy-Guided Error Mitigation for Quantum Reinforcement Learning on a Real-World CVRP Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18219)
*Shabir Ahmad Sofi,Bisma Majid,Mir Mohammad Yousuf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应策略引导误差缓解（APGEM），根据学习上下文动态选择零噪声外推（ZNE）、概率误差补偿（PEC）、克利福德数据回归（CDR）和读出误差缓解（REM）四种方法，在真实城市物流测试平台上对带容量约束的车辆路径问题（CVRP）进行评估，结果显示在高噪声下近似比从0.84-0.87提升至0.92-0.94，且控制器随训练时长从以CDR为主转向多方法均衡部署，验证了学习感知误差缓解的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在嘈杂中等规模量子（NISQ）硬件上，退相干导致保真度下降并破坏学习稳定性，传统静态误差缓解方法缺乏对学习上下文的响应能力，亟需一种动态、学习感知的误差缓解机制以支持量子强化学习（QRL）在组合优化中的实际应用。

Method: 设计了一个基于保真度、熵和成本感知的效用函数，结合epsilon-greedy规则，通过时序差分Q值在线决策选用ZNE、PEC、CDR或REM四种误差缓解技术，实现自适应选择；采用真实地理数据构建德里城市物流场景下的CVRP实例，覆盖五种噪声类型和四个严重程度级别进行测试。

Result: QRL代理在性能上优于构造启发式方法，并接近元启发式算法；在高噪声条件下，误差缓解使近似比从0.84–0.87显著提升至0.92–0.94；控制器在短训练周期以CDR为主，长周期则实现四类方法的均衡部署，体现真正的依赖于训练阶段的智能选择机制。

Conclusion: 自适应、学习感知的误差缓解是实现噪声鲁棒量子强化学习的可行路径，为未来在真实量子硬件上开展复杂优化任务提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: Quantum Reinforcement Learning (QRL) represents policies as variational quantum circuits (VQCs), making it attractive for combinatorial optimization such as the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (CVRP). On noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) hardware, however, decoherence degrades fidelity and destabilizes learning, and conventional error mitigation is applied statically without regard to the learning context. We introduce Adaptive Policy-Guided Error Mitigation (APGEM), a controller that selects among Zero-Noise Extrapolation (ZNE), Probabilistic Error Cancellation (PEC), Clifford Data Regression (CDR), and Readout Error Mitigation (REM) online, driven by a fidelity, entropy, and cost aware utility function and an epsilon-greedy rule over temporal-difference Q-scores. We evaluate on a realistic urban-logistics testbed, a Delhi-based CVRP over real landmarks with geodesic inter-node costs, exercised across five noise families and four severity levels. On this instance, the QRL agent outperforms constructive heuristics and approaches metaheuristics, while mitigation restores approximation ratios from 0.84-0.87 to 0.92-0.94 under high noise. The controller shifts from a CDR-dominated regime under short training horizons to a balanced deployment across all four techniques under longer horizons, indicating genuine regime-dependent selection. These preliminary results position adaptive, learning-aware mitigation as a practical route to noise-resilient QRL.

</details>


### [244] [Anomaly Detection in General Ledger Data: Results from a Hybrid Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18228)
*Jan Gronewald,Alexander Michael Rombach,Sebastian Stephan,Peter Fettke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了将审计中的日记账测试（JETs）与机器学习（ML）方法结合的混合策略，旨在减少假阳性，提高异常检测的准确性和审计效率。实验基于合成数据，包含正常和异常的日记账条目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JETs虽能识别已知模式，但结果列表过长且包含大量假阳性，增加审计工作量。为提升审计经济效率，需减少假阳性，机器学习方法被视为改善异常检测的有效途径。

Method: 提出专门的机器学习模型，结合JETs与ML方法，以增强异常检测性能和结果有效性，采用合成数据进行实验验证。

Result: 实验表明，结合JETs与机器学习的混合方法能有效提升异常检测的准确性，降低假阳性率，从而提高审计效率。

Conclusion: 将JETs与机器学习相结合的混合方法在提升审计异常检测能力方面具有显著潜力，有助于实现更高效、经济的审计过程。

Abstract: Journal Entry Tests (JETs) are a mandatory part of annual audits to evaluate and assess both highrisk audit areas and potential material misstatements. However, as JETs are designed to detect known patterns based on domain knowledge, the resulting lists are often very large and require substantial additional effort from the auditor. To ensure the economic efficiency of the audit, the number of false positives in JET result lists must be reduced. Especially machine learning (ML) methods represent a promising approach to improve anomaly detection in this field. In this research in progress paper, we investigate different approaches on how to combine JETs with ML-methods in a hybrid manner. We present specialized models to increase the detection performance and validity of anomaly detection results to improve audit efficiency. The experiments are based on synthetic data consisting of different normal and anomalous journal entries.

</details>


### [245] [F-DACE: Fuzzy Disagreement-Aware Causal Evidence Fusion for Abstention-Safe Conversational Retail Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18238)
*Sourish Dey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: F-DACE 是一个基于因果机器学习的决策支持系统，通过融合多种因果估计方法（如DML因果森林、线性回归）并结合诊断工具（如双重差分、置信区间重叠、方向一致性等），在不确定性下做出稳健决策。它采用模糊逻辑处理精度、倾向得分重叠、反事实检验稳定性等因素，并设置硬否决机制以避免错误推荐。在180次面板模拟中，其决策率达67.2%，错误推荐率仅17.2%，优于单一模型。零售应用中对沃尔玛数据的分析显示其对五种促销指标均选择不推荐，因存在估计不精确或符号冲突等问题。配套的LangGraph对话代理在真实与对抗性问题上表现出高准确率和强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观测决策系统常仅依赖单一因果估计作为推荐，忽视不同方法间的分歧，导致潜在误判。本文旨在构建一个能整合多源因果估计、具备自我验证能力且在不确定时主动拒绝推荐的智能决策系统，提升推荐可靠性与可解释性。

Method: 采用因果机器学习框架，以背门调整识别条件平均处理效应（CATE），使用EconML DML因果森林和DoWhy线性回归进行估计，通过两路固定效应模型进行校验，再经约束优化生成候选干预措施。决策层F-DACE将精度、倾向重叠、反事实稳定性、区间重叠与方向一致性转化为模糊隶属度，并引入硬否决规则（如估计不一致、诊断失败、显著符号冲突或证据弱）来强制系统在不可靠情况下放弃推荐。

Result: 在180次面板模拟中，F-DACE在67.2%的场景中做出决策，错误推荐率为17.2%，显著优于因果森林（33.3%）和后门回归（35.6%）的确定性一致策略。几乎所有错误推荐（30/31）发生在共享未观测混杂条件下，表明该系统无法检测所有组件共有的遗漏变量问题。零售案例中，对45家门店6,435个店周的数据分析显示，五个促销指标均被拒绝推荐，原因包括估计不精确、反事实检验失败及符号冲突。对话代理在24个真实问题上实现100%工具路由准确率、100%状态保真度和0.983的平均可信度；在10个对抗性问题中完全抵御了恶意指令。

Conclusion: F-DACE通过融合多个因果估计器与多重诊断机制，实现了在复杂不确定性下的可靠决策，在保持高可信度的同时有效降低错误推荐风险。其设计体现了‘宁可不决策，也不出错’的原则，适用于对安全性要求高的实际应用场景，如零售营销决策。

Abstract: Observational decision-support systems often expose one causal estimate as a recommendation even when plausible estimators disagree. The inherent engine of the proposed system is causal machine learning: a conditional-average-treatment-effect estimand identified by backdoor adjustment, estimated by an EconML DML causal forest and DoWhy linear regression, checked by two-way fixed effects, and converted into candidate levers by constrained optimisation. F-DACE is the decision layer on that engine. It represents precision, propensity overlap, placebo-refutation stability, interval overlap, and directional agreement as fuzzy memberships. Hard vetoes force abstention after estimand mismatch, failed diagnostics, informative sign conflict, or weak evidence. In 180 panel simulations spanning six identification conditions, F-DACE made a decision in 67.2% of runs and limited false recommendations to 17.2%; the corresponding rates were 33.3% for the causal forest and 35.6% for backdoor regression, matching deterministic unanimity rather than dominating it. Nearly all (30 of 31) false recommendations occurred under shared unmeasured confounding, which no fusion rule can diagnose when every component shares the omitted variable. The retail application aggregates a public Walmart panel to 6,435 store-weeks across 45 stores. F-DACE abstains for all five markdown indicators: some estimates are imprecise, one refutation fails, and MarkDown5 has a direct sign conflict. A LangGraph conversational agent exposes impact, what-if, and lever-optimization tools while a deterministic verifier preserves causal-layer status. On 24 live questions it achieved 100.0% tool-routing accuracy, 100.0% status fidelity, and 0.983 mean groundedness. On ten adversarial questions it resisted all injected instructions.

</details>


### [246] [Behavioral Fingerprinting and Navigation Prediction in Web Browsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18273)
*Ralph Elsaghbini,Omran Berjawi,Walid Fahs,Rida Khatoun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究对比分析了两种互补的网络行为推断任务：会话级用户识别和下一域名预测。基于清洗后的事件流数据，在大规模匿名浏览痕迹上进行评估，会话划分与数据分割根据任务需求调整。用户识别任务采用经典与神经网络模型，基于会话级行为和域名特征；下一域名预测则结合图模型与大语言模型（LLM）。实验表明，短浏览会话具有高度可识别性，而未来导航行为可由长期交互结构与近期行为上下文有效预测。然而，LLM提取的语义特征仅带来微弱增益，说明重复的交互模式仍是主要预测信号。结果凸显了交互历史在用户识别与导航预测中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索短时间浏览行为中蕴含的丰富结构化行为信号，揭示用户识别与导航预测的潜在规律，评估不同模型在真实场景下的表现。

Method: 对同一清洗后的事件流数据，分别构建会话级用户识别与下一域名预测任务；前者使用经典与神经模型处理会话级行为与域名特征；后者结合图建模与大语言模型（LLM）进行预测。任务设计考虑各自的时间特性，实现针对性的会话划分与数据拆分。

Result: 短浏览会话具有高度可识别性；未来导航行为可通过长期交互结构与近期行为上下文有效预测；但LLM生成的语义特征带来的性能提升有限，说明重复交互模式是主导预测信号。

Conclusion: 交互历史在网页浏览行为中起决定性作用，不仅显著提升用户识别能力，也增强导航行为的可预测性；尽管引入语义信息，但结构性与序列性特征仍为最核心的预测依据。

Abstract: Web browsing often appears ephemeral: users visit a few websites, complete a task, and move on. However, even short fragments of browsing activity can contain rich and structured behavioral signals. In this work, we conduct a comparative empirical study of two complementary behavioral inference tasks: session-level user identification and next-domain prediction. Both tasks are derived from the same cleaned event stream and evaluated on large-scale anonymous browsing traces, with sessionization and splitting adapted to the temporal requirements of each task. For user identification, we evaluate classical and neural models operating on session-level behavioral and domain features. For next-domain prediction, we combine graph-based modeling with Large Language Models (LLMs). Experimental results show that short browsing sessions are highly identifiable, while future navigation actions are highly predictable from long-term interaction structure combined with recent behavioral context. Furthermore, LLM-derived semantic features yield only marginal gains over purely structural and sequential models, indicating that repeated interaction patterns remain the dominant predictive signal in the evaluated web-browsing setup. These findings highlight the extent to which interaction history substantially contributes to both user identifiability and navigation predictability in browsing traces.

</details>


### [247] [A GAN-Based Framework for Robust DDoS Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18281)
*Makram Chehayeb,Walid Fahs,Amina Rizk,Rida Khatoun,Omran Berjawi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合生成对抗网络与先进机器学习模型的鲁棒检测框架，以应对复杂演化且能规避传统安全系统的分布式拒绝服务（DDoS）攻击。通过在CICDDoS2019数据集上训练随机森林、深度神经集成和基于Transformer的模型建立基线性能，并利用带有梯度惩罚的Wasserstein生成对抗网络（WGAN-GP）生成模拟对抗性流量，构建混合数据集以增强模型对未知对抗流量的泛化能力。实验表明，该方法显著提升检测准确率与抗干扰能力，且在真实流量测试中表现良好，为未来可扩展、高效的自适应网络安全防御系统提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的网络防御系统难以应对日益复杂的DDoS攻击，尤其是针对机器学习模型的对抗性攻击会严重降低其分类准确性，亟需更鲁棒的检测机制。

Method: 采用WGAN-GP生成合成对抗流量，将其与正常及恶意流量混合，用于训练多种先进机器学习模型（如随机森林、深度神经集成、Transformer），从而提升模型对未见对抗样本的识别能力。

Result: 所提方法在多个评估指标上显著提升检测精度与鲁棒性，尤其在面对未知对抗流量时表现优异；真实环境流量测试验证了其实际应用潜力。

Conclusion: 该融合生成对抗增强与先进学习模型的框架为构建更具韧性与适应性的网络防御系统提供了有效解决方案，具有良好的可扩展性与实用性。

Abstract: The availability and consistency of online services remain vulnerable due to Distributed Denial of Service (DDoS) attacks. These attacks are evolving by adopting more complex strategies to evade traditional network security systems. Despite the effectiveness of machine learning models in detecting DDoS traffic, targeted adversarial attacks can degrade their classification accuracy. This work proposes a robust detection framework that integrates generative adversarial modelling with advanced machine learning models. We trained Random Forests, Deep Neural Ensembles, and Transformer-based models using the CICDDoS2019 dataset to establish the frameworks baseline performance. To enhance the models defensive capacity, we generated synthetic adversarial flows that simulate potential evasion attempts and adversarial traffic using a Wasserstein Generative Adversarial Network with Gradient Penalty (WGAN-GP). Then, we combined the generated traffic with benign and malicious traffic to construct hybrid datasets to train the models to learn more generalizable decision boundaries. The experimental results indicate that the proposed methodology significantly enhances detection accuracy and resilience, especially against unseen adversarial traffic. We also tested the designed framework using real-world generated traffic, which demonstrates its capability in practical settings. The scalable and efficient solution against adversarial DDoS attacks, introduced in this work, paves the way towards more resilient and adaptive network defense systems that combine generative adversarial augmentation with recent advances in learning models.

</details>


### [248] [Bias Amplification in Multi-Agent Network: How Biased Agents Shape Opinions and Rhetoric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18306)
*Omran Berjawi,Giuseppe Fenza,Rida Khatoun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在大型语言模型（LLMs）构成的多智能体系统中，偏见如何通过文本交互传播。即使少数智能体持有极端观点，也会显著影响其他非偏见智能体的观点演化。研究发现，与经典Friedkin-Johnsen（FJ）模型相比，Llama 3.2模型在相同偏见比例下更快地出现观点转变。语义分析显示，随着受到偏见暴露的增加，文本解释中的修辞一致性系统性上升，且这种一致性部分脱离数值上的观点收敛——即非偏见智能体虽未大幅改变数值观点，却逐渐采用偏见智能体的表达方式。研究揭示了偏见与语言在多智能体语言模型生态系统中协同演化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在多智能体系统中广泛应用，但其内部偏见传播机制仍不清晰。尤其在涉及集体推理与决策的情境下，理解偏见如何通过文本交互扩散至关重要。本文旨在揭示偏见在语言互动中的传播路径及其对语言表达的影响。

Method: 构建一个由多个大语言模型组成的多智能体系统，其中一小部分智能体持续持有极端观点，其余智能体通过结构化文本交互逐步更新自身信念。通过模拟不同比例的偏见智能体，分析其对整体观点演化的影响，并结合语义分析评估文本表达的一致性与词汇借用现象。

Result: 少量偏见智能体即可引发非偏见智能体显著的观点变化；在同等条件下，Llama 3.2模型比经典FJ模型更快实现观点转移。语义层面，偏见暴露导致修辞一致性增强，且非偏见智能体在数值观点变化不大时，已开始使用偏见智能体的词汇和表达方式，表明语言与观点的演变存在解耦现象。

Conclusion: 偏见不仅影响观点数值，还深刻重塑语言表达。在多智能体语言模型系统中，语言与偏见共同演化，即使轻微的偏见输入也可能导致系统性语言同化。这提示需关注语言层面的偏见传播，而不仅仅是观点数值的变化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in applications involving interaction between agents, where their output plays a role in collective reasoning and decision-making processes. Despite significant research into the functioning of LLMs in such multi-agent systems, the processes of bias propagation in such systems are still a challenge. This work studies how biased opinions are propagated in the form of textual interaction in an environment of LLMs, in which a minority of agents maintain persistent extreme opinions, while the remaining agents iteratively update their beliefs through structured textual interactions. The findings show that even the presence of a small percentage of biased agents in such a system leads to significant shifts in the opinions of non-biased agents. It suggests that for the same percentage of biased agents, the shifts occur more quickly for the Llama~3.2 model when compared to a classical Friedkin-Johnsen (FJ) model. Further semantic analysis demonstrates that rhetorical consistency in textual explanations increases systematically with biased exposure and, importantly, is partially decoupled from numerical convergenumericalutral agents adopt the vocabulary employed by the biased agents even in configurations where their numerical opinion shifts remain moderate. The research helps explain how bias and language develop together in multi-agent language model ecosystems.

</details>


### [249] [Beyond Quadratic Loss: The Stability Phase Diagram of Adam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18314)
*Gaoxiang Tang,Huanran Chen,Ziming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Adam优化器在神经网络训练中出现的损失尖峰现象，揭示其与两个动量参数β₁和β₂之间的关系。通过在(β₁, β₂)平面上映射训练动态，发现存在一条近似线性边界1−β₂ = C(1−β₁)，将有尖峰与无尖峰的动态区分开来。对于不同类型的损失函数，该边界斜率与损失的有效指数相关，特别是针对交叉熵损失的‘核心-墙’结构，表现出超二次行为，解释了尖峰产生的几何根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Adam优化器中损失尖峰的成因，尤其是其两个动量时间尺度（β₁, β₂）如何共同影响训练稳定性，从而为优化器设计提供理论支持。

Method: 在多个模型-任务设置下，系统地扫描(β₁, β₂)参数平面，分析训练过程中的损失动态；结合一维和高维损失函数（如|x|^n）的数学建模，以及对交叉熵损失的几何结构分析（核心-墙景观），揭示损失尖峰的机制。

Result: 发现损失尖峰区域与(β₁, β₂)之间存在近似线性边界，且边界斜率由损失函数的有效指数决定；实际的交叉熵损失展现出类似超二次的几何特性，导致优化器更新尺度下的不稳定行为。

Conclusion: Adam优化器的损失尖峰可归因于动量时间尺度不匹配与有限尺度下损失函数的超二次几何结构，尤其在交叉熵损失中体现为‘核心-墙’景观，这为理解并缓解训练不稳定性提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Loss spikes are recurrent instabilities in neural-network training and can arise from multiple mechanisms. For Adam in particular, macroscopic loss spikes have been linked to optimizer dynamics, yet how its two momentum timescales govern them remains unclear. We investigate this dependence by mapping training dynamics across the $(β_1,β_2)$ plane. Across a range of model--task settings, an approximately linear boundary, $1-β_2=C(1-β_1)$, separates spiky from non-spiky dynamics, whereas a one-dimensional quadratic loss produces approximately cubic slope. A one-dimensional superquadratic loss $L(x)\propto|x|^n$ recovers the near-linear scaling and links the boundary coefficient to the effective loss exponent $n$. We further show that confident cross-entropy losses develop a core--wall landscape comprising a narrow quadratic core followed by a steep wall, which produces effective superquadratic behavior at the scale of an optimizer update. Together, these results connect Adam loss spikes to both the mismatch between momentum timescales and finite-scale superquadratic loss geometry beyond the Hessian.

</details>


### [250] [Multi-Appliance Non-Intrusive Load Monitoring via Label-Preserving Aggregate Recomposition and Prediction Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18315)
*Jiangfeng Liu,Yanfang Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于标签保持的聚合重组和预测一致性方法，通过双阶段共享到特定的专家混合路由架构，在不增加推理时模块或参数的情况下，提升了非侵入式负载监测（NILM）在未见家庭中的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NILM模型在源家庭上训练后，在未见家庭中准确率下降，且聚合功率受其他电器和测量误差影响，导致预测依赖于与目标共现的背景残留。为解决此问题，提出利用时间对齐的子计量数据和聚合功率的加性分解，使聚合窗口可通过替换其背景残留而保留目标电器功率序列。

Method: 结合标签保持的聚合重组与预测一致性，两个窗口均获得完整的功率和运行状态监督；仅当两个预测均满足固定可靠性标准且差异超过固定阈值时，才对分歧进行惩罚。采用多电器架构，使用两阶段共享到特定的专家混合路由机制实现。

Result: 在REDD、UK-DALE和REFIT数据集上，相对于单窗口训练，设备平均绝对误差分别从14.75降至13.14 W，8.88降至8.51 W，15.83降至14.55 W。该方法仅在训练阶段使用，不影响推理阶段。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过利用聚合功率的可重构性与预测一致性，显著提升了跨家庭场景下非侵入式负载监测的泛化性能，且无需额外推理开销。

Abstract: Non-intrusive load monitoring (NILM) estimates appliance power sequences from aggregate power, but models trained on source households commonly lose accuracy in unseen households. Aggregate power also contains loads from other appliances and measurement error, so predictions may depend on the residual background that co-occurs with source-household targets. Time-aligned submetered measurements and the additive decomposition of aggregate power expose a relation unused by window-wise supervision: an aggregate window can be recomposed by replacing only its residual background while preserving all modeled target-appliance power sequences pointwise. We combine label-preserving aggregate recomposition with prediction consistency. Both windows receive complete power and operating-state supervision. For each appliance, disagreement between the two power predictions is penalized only when both satisfy a fixed reliability criterion and only to the extent that it exceeds a fixed margin. The proposed method is implemented using a multi-appliance architecture with two-stage shared-to-specific mixture-of-experts routing. On REDD, UK-DALE, and REFIT, the proposed method lowers appliance-averaged mean absolute error relative to single-window training from 14.75 to 13.14 W, from 8.88 to 8.51 W, and from 15.83 to 14.55 W. Label-preserving aggregate recomposition and prediction consistency are used only during training, and add no inference-time module or parameter.

</details>


### [251] [Trajectory Learnability for Offline On-Policy Distillation with Imperfect Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18321)
*Yihao Ai,Weilong Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种新的离线策略蒸馏方法，通过利用教师成功问题作为参考，计算学生在教师失败轨迹中各token的似然变化，生成可学习性信号，进而加权原始蒸馏损失。该方法无需额外生成，效率高，仅需2个GPU和约22小时，相比在线方法更高效，并在数学推理和代码生成任务上显著提升性能，最高提升2.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线策略蒸馏虽高效，但因重复使用不完美的教师监督，导致错误信息持续影响学习。尽管教师失败是粗粒度信号，但并不意味着所有相关轨迹都无价值。因此，研究如何从不完美监督中提取仍可学习的部分成为关键挑战。

Method: 提出基于教师成功问题构建参考基准，训练学生模型仅在教师成功问题上进行；然后评估在教师失败轨迹中各token的似然变化，以符号化变化量作为可学习性信号；将该信号聚合为轨迹级权重，用于调整原始蒸馏损失。整个过程无需重新生成轨迹，仅依赖已有存储数据与模型检查点。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成多个基准上，该方法优于离线OPD基线，最高提升2.7个百分点，且达到或超过在线OPD方法表现；同时仅需2个GPU和约22小时计算资源，远低于在线方法的3个GPU和36-48小时。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法有效识别并利用了从教师失败轨迹中仍可学习的信息，通过低成本、无需重生成的可学习性信号机制，在保持高效率的同时显著提升离线策略蒸馏性能。

Abstract: Offline on-policy distillation gains efficiency by collecting student trajectories and teacher supervision once and reusing them throughout optimization. The same reuse makes imperfect supervision persistent. Since even strong teachers can fail, we ask \emph{what remains learnable from imperfect teacher supervision?} Teacher failure is only a coarse problem-level signal and does not imply that all supervision along the associated student trajectory is unhelpful. A natural alternative is to estimate teacher recoverability along the trajectory, but repeated continuations largely erase the efficiency advantage of offline distillation. We instead use teacher-successful problems to define a cheap reference for what the student can learn. We train on teacher-successful problems and measure how the likelihood of each observed token in trajectories from teacher-failed problems changes. We use these signed likelihood changes as an operational \emph{learnability signal}: larger increases indicate behavior more strongly promoted by successful-only learning. We aggregate this signal into trajectory-level weights for the original distillation loss. Unlike continuation-based estimates, our learnability requires no additional generation and can be computed once from stored trajectories and model checkpoints. Across mathematical reasoning and code generation, our method improves an offline OPD baseline by up to 2.7 percentage points and matches or outperforms online OPD variants on multiple benchmarks. Despite the additional successful-only distillation stage, it uses 2 GPUs and about 22 GPU hours, compared with 3 GPUs and 36--48 GPU hours for representative online OPD methods.

</details>


### [252] [Every Fixed Metric Has a Blind Spot: A Learned Atmospheric Critic for Scoring Forecast Realism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18381)
*Younes Elberkennou,Dmitri Demler,Thierry Meier,Luca Rispoli,Fanny Lehmann,Joel Oskarsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于判别器的气象预报模型真实性评估方法，通过训练判别器区分真实气象数据与模型输出，利用其输出得分作为类散度的真实性指标。该方法能自适应检测模型的各种失真模式，优于传统固定表示的评估指标，在合成扰动和真实模型预测中均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有气象预报评估指标依赖于预先设定的数据表示或变换，难以全面检测模型的各种失真模式（如模糊、周期性异常等），限制了对机器学习模型失败模式的识别能力。

Method: 训练一个判别器以区分真实气象数据（如ERA5再分析数据）与模型生成的输出，并利用判别器的logit输出构建一个类散度的现实性评分，使该评分能够自适应地捕捉模型所表现出的任何失真特征。

Result: 在多种合成扰动实验中，该方法成功识别并正确排序了不同类型的扰动严重程度，而现有指标至少在一种扰动下失效；在真实天气模型评估中，该现实性评分随预报时效增加而下降，且数值模型普遍获得比机器学习模型更高的评分。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于判别器的学习型大气评判器能有效捕捉机器学习气象模型的多种非物理解释性失真，具备更强的适应性和检测能力，是一种更全面、灵活的模型评估工具。

Abstract: Despite their high accuracy on point-wise metrics, machine learning weather forecasting models can exhibit different failure modes such as blurring, periodic irregularities, and other unphysical spatial artifacts. This has motivated a variety of metrics to detect known failure cases. Existing metrics fix a representation or transformation in advance, and that choice limits the artifacts they can detect. We propose to train a discriminator for separating reference data from the model's output, and using its output logit to obtain a divergence-like realism score. The discriminator learns whatever separates the model's fields from real weather, adapting to whichever failure mode that model exhibits. We compare our learned atmospheric critic to existing metrics using various synthetic corruptions applied to ERA5 reanalysis data. Our method successfully identifies the corruptions and ranks their severity, while existing metrics fail on at least one corruption. Additionally, we evaluate forecasts from real weather models, and find that the realism score degrades with longer lead times and the metric generally assigns higher realism to numerical models than to machine learning models.

</details>


### [253] [Reliable Virtual Sensing: A Multi-Domain Benchmark for Robustness Under Sensor Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18396)
*Jens U. Brandt,Noah C. Puetz,Alexander Windmann,Marc Hilbert,Elena Raponi,Thomas Bäck,Thomas Bartz-Beielstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MuViS-C是首个针对学习型虚拟传感中常见传感器故障的鲁棒性多领域基准，涵盖十种故障模式及多种鲁棒性度量。在九个来自六个领域的数据集上评估了六种模型架构，发现所有模型在故障下均显著退化，梯度提升树集成表现最佳，而专门的鲁棒性增强策略可缩小与最稳健模型的差距，但会降低正常性能。多领域设计至关重要，因模型排名随数据集变化，单一领域无法全面反映鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界部署要求学习型虚拟传感系统具备对传感器故障的鲁棒性，现有方法在传感器失效时可能产生物理上不合理的估计，导致系统级故障，因此需要一个全面的基准来评估和改进鲁棒性。

Method: 基于已有基准和已建立的损坏分类法，构建覆盖十种传感器故障模式（从轻微漂移到灾难性信号丢失）的多严重性测试集，并结合平均误差、相对退化和最坏情况脆弱性等互补鲁棒性度量。在九个跨六个领域的数据集上，对六种序列建模架构（包括梯度提升树、卷积、循环、注意力和MLP混合）进行评估，并对注意力模型进一步测试三种鲁棒性增强策略。

Result: 所有模型在传感器故障下均出现显著退化，至少在一个故障设置下表现劣于朴素预测器；梯度提升树集成展现出强鲁棒性；专用鲁棒性增强策略能缩小注意力模型与其他稳健模型之间的差距，但会损害其正常性能；多领域设计揭示模型排名随数据集变化，单一领域无法全面反映鲁棒性表现。

Conclusion: MuViS-C为学习型虚拟传感的鲁棒性评估提供了首个多领域基准，证明了多领域评估的重要性，且其开放源代码和可扩展性有助于未来研究和应用发展。

Abstract: Virtual sensing, the estimation of hard-to-measure quantities from available sensor measurements, is a critical enabler for control and monitoring in cyber-physical systems. However, when sensors fail, learning-based predictors can produce physically implausible estimates that propagate to system-level failures. We argue that real-world deployment demands robustness and introduce MuViS-C, the first multi-domain benchmark of robustness against common sensor failures in learning-based virtual sensing. Building on an existing nominal-performance benchmark and established corruption taxonomies, it covers ten sensor failure modes, from subtle drifts to catastrophic signal dropouts, at multiple severities. These are paired with complementary robustness measures capturing average error under corruption, relative degradation, and worst-case fragility. Across nine datasets from six domains, we benchmark six architectures spanning gradient-boosted trees and the major inductive biases for sequence modeling: convolution, recurrence, attention, and MLP-mixing. On the attention-based architecture, we further probe three robustification strategies. We find that (i) every model degrades substantially under corruption, becoming worse than a naïve predictor on at least one corruption setting, (ii) gradient-boosted tree ensembles achieve strong robustness, and (iii) dedicated robustification closes the gap between the attention-based architecture and the most robust models, though each strategy hurts nominal performance. The benchmark's multi-domain design proves essential, as model rankings shift across datasets, and no single domain captures the full robustness picture. MuViS-C is open-source and extensible to new datasets, failure modes, measures, and models.

</details>


### [254] [TERN: A Delta-rule Memory with a Seasonal Reference and Online Adaptation for Epidemic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18407)
*Shunya Nagashima,Yuta Funayama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TERN提出一种基于通道自适应衰减和学习地址擦除的快速权重记忆机制，结合显式季节参考与在线适应，有效处理流感流行周期中信息时效性变化问题，在多个流感预测基准上表现优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流行病图模型和通用预测器无法充分利用历史季节数据，也无法在流行阶段变化时及时丢弃过时信息，导致预测性能受限。

Method: 设计了一种基于delta规则的快速权重记忆模块，该模块通过局部流行相位特征驱动的门控机制实现通道级衰减与学习地址擦除，并融合显式季节参考与在线更新能力。

Result: 在三个Cola-GNN流感预测基准上，TERN优于现有流行病图模型和通用预测器，达到或超过季节性参考模型的性能，控制实验验证了记忆模块的有效性。

Conclusion: TERN通过动态记忆机制有效捕捉流感传播中的时序演化规律，显著提升短期流行趋势预测精度，为公共卫生决策提供更可靠支持。

Abstract: Weekly influenza surveillance counts guide vaccine distribution and public-health alerts, yet they are hard to forecast. Each region offers only a few seasons, waves shift in timing and height every year, and information that helps while a wave grows misleads after its peak, whereas last season's shape stays informative for a year. Existing epidemic graph models and general forecasters read a short fixed window and treat all past information alike, so they neither exploit earlier seasons nor discard stale associations when the epidemic phase changes. To address these limitations, we propose TERN, a forecaster built around a delta-rule fast-weight memory that decays channel-wise and erases along a learned address under gates driven by local epidemic-phase features, combined with an explicit seasonal reference and online adaptation. On three Cola-GNN influenza benchmarks, TERN outperformed epidemic graph models and general forecasters, matched or exceeded seasonal references, and a controlled comparison confirmed the contribution of the memory itself.

</details>


### [255] [Spatially Adaptive Noise Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18466)
*Frantzeska Lavda,Maciej Falkiewicz,Van Khoa Nguyen,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为空间自适应噪声注入（SANI）的新采样框架，通过在每个像素级别动态调整噪声注入，以更好地适应自然图像的几何结构。与传统方法中全局统一的噪声注入不同，SANI根据图像局部特性智能决定是否在特定位置注入噪声：边缘和纹理等高曲率区域受益于随机校正，而平滑区域则避免噪声干扰。该方法结合概率门控机制和空间自适应方差，显著提升了生成质量，在多种采样步数下均优于标准DDPM和DDIM，并与基于方差学习的先进方法相当，证明了空间自适应在扩散采样中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散采样方法（如DDPM和DDIM）采用全局统一的噪声注入策略，忽略了自然图像中不同区域（如边缘与平滑区域）对噪声敏感度的差异。高曲率区域（如边缘、纹理）因模型不确定性需要随机校正，而平滑区域则易被噪声破坏，因此亟需一种能够根据空间位置自适应调节噪声注入的机制。

Method: 提出空间自适应噪声注入（SANI）框架，引入一个基于图像局部特征的概率门控机制，动态决定每个像素在每一步是否接受噪声注入；同时设计空间自适应方差，使噪声强度随空间位置变化，仅在必要区域注入噪声，从而保留清晰结构并增强复杂细节。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，SANI在不同采样步数下均显著优于标准DDPM和DDIM，FID指标持续提升；同时与先进的方差学习方法相比保持竞争力，表明空间自适应在扩散采样中具有关键作用。

Conclusion: 空间自适应噪声注入（SANI）通过精准控制噪声在图像空间中的分布，有效提升了扩散模型的生成质量，验证了根据图像局部几何特性动态调整噪声注入的重要性，为未来扩散采样方法的设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Diffusion samplers reverse a learned noising process using either stochastic (DDPM) or deterministic (DDIM) updates, which represent endpoints of a single family controlled by a scalar noise-injection variance that is applied identically at every spatial location. This uniform approach neglects the geometry of natural images: high-curvature regions such as edges and textures, where the denoiser is uncertain, benefit from stochastic correction, whereas smooth regions, where the score is precise, are degraded by injected noise. This work investigates whether each pixel requires stochastic correction at a given timestep and introduces Spatially Adaptive Noise Injection (SANI), a novel sampling framework that dynamically adjusts noise application on a per-pixel basis. SANI integrates a probabilistic gating mechanism with a derived spatially adaptive variance, ensuring that noise is injected precisely where needed to refine complex features while preserving well-formed structures. Experimental results and decoupling ablations demonstrate that SANI consistently improves Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) over the vanilla DDPM and DDIM endpoint samplers across diverse sampling timesteps, while remaining competitive with variance-learning baselines, highlighting the importance of spatial adaptivity in diffusion sampling.

</details>


### [256] [Provable Guarantees for Spectral Structured Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18527)
*Violet Zheng,Jean Honorio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在带有边翻转噪声的符号图上通过简单的谱方法进行二元节点标签恢复的问题，该方法从噪声符号邻接矩阵的主特征向量的符号中解码节点标签。作者利用矩阵集中理论和特征向量扰动分析工具，推导出新的集中不等式，明确量化了邻接矩阵的谱间隙、节点数量、度分布和噪声水平的影响。理论结果与图的Cheeger常数相关联，并针对不同图类提供了具体结论。同时，通过合成实验验证了理论的有效性。本文首次为基于谱的方法提供了理论保证，并衍生出对其他机器学习问题可能具有独立价值的技术结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在带噪声的符号图上进行节点标签恢复时缺乏充分的理论支持，尤其对于基于谱的简单方法缺少系统性分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立结构无关的理论框架，以理解谱方法在噪声环境下的性能边界。

Method: 采用谱方法，即从噪声符号邻接矩阵的主特征向量的符号中推断节点标签；结合矩阵集中理论与特征向量扰动分析，推导出关于标签近似恢复和最大角度偏差的理论保证。

Result: 提出了新的集中不等式，揭示了谱间隙、节点数、度分布和噪声水平对恢复精度的影响；将结果与图的Cheeger常数关联，适用于多种图类；合成实验验证了理论预测的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文首次为基于谱的符号图节点标签恢复方法提供了全面的理论保障，所提出的分析框架不仅适用于当前任务，其技术成果还可推广至其他机器学习场景。

Abstract: Structured prediction is the simultaneous prediction of multiple labels, and is widely used in various fields, such as natural language processing and computer vision. In this paper, we study binary node label recovery on signed graphs with edge-flip noise, a model introduced by (Globerson et al., 2015), via a simple spectral method that decodes node labels from the signs of the principal eigenvector of the noisy signed adjacency matrix. We develop graph structure-agnostic theoretical guarantees for approximate inference of node labels as well as guarantees for maximum angle deviation with respect to the ground truth node labels. By leveraging tools from matrix concentration theory and eigenvector perturbation analysis, we derive new concentration inequalities that explicitly quantify the effect of the spectral gap of the adjacency matrix, number of nodes, degree distribution, and noise level. As a corollary, we relate our general results to the Cheeger constant and provide results for different classes of graphs. We perform several synthetic experiments to validate our theory. To the best of our knowledge, we are the first to provide theoretical guarantees for the spectral-based approach. As a byproduct of our analysis, we derive technical results that might be of independent interest and useful for other machine learning problems.

</details>


### [257] [Provable Guarantees and Efficient Learning of Structural Equation Models with Latent Confounders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18535)
*Weijian Yu,Jean Honorio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究存在潜在混杂因素的线性结构方程模型，提出一种迭代识别终端（可观测）节点并重构可观测变量有向无环图的算法。通过将可观测变量的精度矩阵分解为稀疏加低秩矩阵：稀疏部分捕捉可观测变量间的条件依赖关系，低秩部分捕捉少数潜在混杂因素的联合影响。理论证明在样本量 $n \gtrsim \max\{s\log p,\ r p\}$ 时，该方法能正确识别可观测变量间的因果关系。实验验证了理论结果的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在潜在混杂因素的情况下，传统因果发现方法容易产生虚假关联和错误边方向，因此需要有效处理潜在混杂因素以准确恢复因果结构。

Method: 提出一种基于精度矩阵分解的方法，将观测变量的精度矩阵建模为稀疏矩阵与低秩矩阵之和，利用稀疏部分表示可观测变量间的条件依赖，低秩部分表示潜在混杂因素的联合影响，并通过迭代策略识别终端节点和重构有向无环图。

Result: 理论上证明了当样本量满足 $n \gtrsim \max\{s\log p,\ r p\}$ 时，该方法可正确识别可观测变量间的因果关系；实验结果验证了方法的可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法能够有效应对潜在混杂因素的影响，在足够样本下准确恢复可观测变量间的因果结构，为复杂系统中的因果发现提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to recover causal relationships from observed data. In various fields, exploring causal relationships among variables remains an important topic, but this task becomes challenging due to the existence of latent confounders. Ignoring such confounders can lead to false associations and incorrect edge directions. In this paper, we study the linear structural equation model with latent confounders. We propose an algorithm that iteratively identifies terminal (observed) nodes and reconstructs the directed acyclic graph of the observed variables. To do this, we recover the precision matrix of the observed variables as a sparse plus low-rank matrix: a sparse matrix captures the conditional dependencies among observed variables, while a low-rank matrix captures the combined influence of a few latent confounders. We establish that for $p$ observed variables, $r$ latent confounders and $s$ edges, our procedure correctly identifies the directed causal relationship among observed variables, for $n \gtrsim \max\{s\log p,\ r p\}$ samples. Experimental results validate our theoretical contributions.

</details>


### [258] [Revisiting the Objective of Echo Chamber Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18545)
*Abylaikhan Bexeit,Kushani Perera,Shanika Karunasekera,Jean Honorio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究社交网络中回音室的检测，即识别在某个话题上意见一致但与其他节点分歧的一组节点。作者提出了一种基于集合函数傅里叶变换理论的新型目标函数，并设计了可扩展的半定松弛算法，通过内点法和稀疏线性代数求解。实验表明，该方法在小规模合成数据上优于现有方法，且在大规模真实数据集上生成的回音室具有更优的网络特性；此外，在小规模真实数据上，其结果与被封禁用户的意见一致性更高，验证了目标函数的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回音室检测不同于传统的社区发现或图划分问题，现有方法缺乏对回音室特性的明确建模，因此需要一个全新的、合理的优化目标来准确识别回音室结构。

Method: 基于集合函数的傅里叶变换理论构建目标函数，采用半定规划松弛并结合内点法与稀疏线性代数进行高效求解。

Result: 在合成数据上能更好恢复真实回音室；在真实数据上生成的回音室具有更优的网络属性；且与被封禁用户的共识度更高，验证了目标函数的合理性。

Conclusion: 本文首次形式化了回音室检测的目标函数，提出的算法在多个方面表现优异，为回音室分析提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the detection of an echo chamber in a social network, i.e., the identification of a set of nodes that agree on a topic, while disagreeing with the rest of nodes. We argue that this problem is different from other social network analysis problems such as community detection, and from other graph problems such as maximum graph cut and maximum clique. To the best of our knowledge, we are the first to formalize the objective function of echo chamber detection, by using the theory of Fourier transforms of set functions (Stobbe and Krause, 2012). We propose scalable semidefinite relaxation, solved via an interior point method and sparse linear algebra. Experimentally, our algorithm recovers the ground truth echo chamber better than competing methods on small synthetic experiments. Our algorithm produces echo chambers with better network properties than competing methods on large real-world datasets. To independently validate our proposed objective function, we show that our algorithm finds echo chambers with more agreements with suspended users than competing methods on a small real-world dataset.

</details>


### [259] [Interpretable Patch-Based Deep Learning for Wildfire Spread Prediction from Ensemble Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18555)
*Marcin Lawenda,Aleksandra Krasicka,David Caballero,Luis Torres,Łukasz Szustak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究比较了四种深度学习模型在模拟加泰罗尼亚地区野火蔓延方面的表现，发现仅地表燃料负荷对燃烧概率有显著预测能力（r=0.27），引入该变量可降低21%的预测误差。卷积模型主要依赖于与火线的距离，而Swin-Unet更关注燃料和地形特征。模型在未重新训练的情况下应用于第二个区域（Pedriza）时仍能有效预测，但准确率略有下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理模拟器虽具可解释性，但计算成本高，因此探索深度学习代理模型能否以更低成本高效复现其结果。

Method: 在2米分辨率下使用10,584次野火模拟数据训练四种模型：基于补丁的U-Net、迁移学习的ResNet-50、基于物理约束的网络以及Swin-Unet Transformer，并通过显著性、遮挡和旋转实验分析模型学习机制。

Result: 燃料负荷是唯一具有较强预测力的变量；引入后可降低21%的预测误差；卷积模型主要依赖火线距离，而Swin-Unet更关注燃料与地形；跨区域应用时三类卷积模型保持预测能力，但准确率略有系统性下降。

Conclusion: 深度学习模型可高效替代物理模拟器进行野火蔓延预测，尤其在跨区域应用中具备一定泛化能力，但模型性能受燃料负荷等关键变量影响显著。

Abstract: Wildfire spread is traditionally predicted using physics-based simulators, which are physically interpretable but whose cost increases with each additional ensemble member. We ask how well deep learning surrogates can reproduce these simulations at a fraction of this cost, training them on 10,584 fire spread simulations at 2m resolution for the Rectoret region in Catalonia, Spain. Four architectures are compared: a patch-based U-Net, a transfer-learned ResNet-50, a physics-informed network constrained by the wind-driven advection equation and a Swin-Unet transformer. Among the terrain and vegetation variables, only surface fuel load predicts burn probability with any strength (r = 0.27) and including it lowers prediction error by 21%. The remaining variables correlate weakly and are highly duplicative. Next, an experiment with saliency, occlusion and rotation demonstrates the models' learning. Convolutional models rely primarily on distance from the current fire front, while Swin-Unet assigns more weight to fuel and terrain, a finding also noted in an unrelated wildfire dataset. When applied without retraining to the second region, Pedriza, all three convolutional models still predict fire spread, losing accuracy by a small but systematic margin.

</details>


### [260] [The evolution of sex for artificial intelligence: a population-genetic framework for multigenerational model populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18560)
*Giorgio F. Gilestro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将AI模型的迭代发展类比为生物种群的演化过程，提出多代模型可视为有性与无性繁殖的混合系统。通过理论建模和实证验证（包括递归训练、平均权重合并、语言模型融合等），作者发现该框架在多种神经网络架构中均成立，并揭示了数据量绝对值的重要性、父母数量对遗传效果的影响、以及因学习冲突导致的生殖隔离现象。这些发现表明，生物学中的群体遗传学理论可直接应用于人工智能系统的演化分析，为理解模型演化提供统一数学框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索人工智能模型在迭代训练过程中表现出的类似生物种群演化的特征，尤其是多代模型间的继承、变异与重组机制，旨在建立一个能预测和解释模型演化行为的通用理论框架。

Method: 采用群体遗传学中的经典模型（如Wright-Fisher过程）构建最小化学习者模型，结合真实数据作为‘移民’输入；在递归训练、平均权重合并、多模型融合等不同场景下测试类比的有效性；使用循环神经网络、前馈网络、变分自编码器生成器及大语言模型进行实证分析。

Result: 所提出的类比在多种模型架构中均有效：递归训练重现了精确的Wright-Fisher过程；真实数据的绝对数量影响演化结果而非比例；平均权重合并导致遗传优势丧失，符合混合遗传理论；而保留各父代最强贡献的组合方式则实现超越父代的表现（体现Fisher-Muller效应）；当模型学习到冲突范式时，出现生殖隔离，无法再融合。

Conclusion: 人工智能系统的发展本质上具有种群演化特征，其继承机制可被群体遗传学框架精准描述。这一跨学科框架不仅具备解释力，还具备预测能力，为未来智能系统的演化管理提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Some aspects of AI development resemble a population process in which models are specialised, retrained on the output of peers, or combined by averaging weights. These practices lead to generations of models, in the biological sense studied by population genetics. Here, I develop this parallelism and interpret multigenerational model populations in terms of sexual and asexual reproduction, formally recombining the two fields. I test these analogies in an exact inheritance model, in trained networks (recurrent, feedforward and variational autoencoder generators) and in large language models, and show that they hold generally, with some measurable architecture-specific biases.
  Training recursively on model output is known to lead to model collapse, a process previously described as akin to genetic drift; I develop all that follows. A minimal model of a learner retrained on its parent's output reproduces the Wright-Fisher process exactly; verified real data added to each generation play the role of immigration, with the surprising finding that the absolute number of real data samples matters, not their share, exactly as in population genetics. Training a child on the average of its parents' outputs cancels the benefit of having several parents, matching blending inheritance (and reviving Jenkin's objection to Darwin), whereas combining parents so that each keeps its strongest contribution preserves it; merged language-model specialists exceeded every parent across seeds (the Fisher-Muller effect); and lineages become reproductively isolated, losing the ability to merge at all, when they have learned conflicting conventions and not when they have merely drifted apart.
  As AI societies become societies in time as well as in space, a mathematical framework for their inheritance acquires predictive power. Remarkably, that framework can be adapted almost wholesale from biology.

</details>


### [261] [Accurate Trace Estimation with Fewer Random Bits via Recursive TensorSketch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18577)
*Mohammad Azhar Khan,Rameshwar Pratap,Amit Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究隐式矩阵 $\mathbf{A} \in \mathbb{R}^{d^p \times d^p}$ 的迹估计问题，仅可通过矩阵-向量乘积查询访问。针对经典Hutchinson估计器随机比特消耗过高的问题，提出一种基于稀疏哈希的新型估计方法，仅需 $O\big(p (d + m)\log m\big)$ 随机比特，保持无偏性的同时，方差随 $p$ 多项式增长，显著优于现有基于Kronecker结构的估计器（其方差指数增长）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Hutchinson估计器在大规模问题中需要大量随机比特（$O(md^p)$），而现有基于Kronecker结构的变体虽减少随机比特至 $O(mpd)$，但方差随 $p$ 指数增长，限制了其在高维场景下的应用。因此，亟需一种既能大幅降低随机资源消耗，又能控制方差随维度增长的估计方法。

Method: 提出一种基于稀疏哈希与分层构造的新型迹估计器，利用 $p$ 个 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 上的随机向量通过特定稀疏化策略生成 $\mathbb{R}^{d^p}$ 空间中的查询向量，结合哈希技巧实现高效采样，从而显著减少随机比特使用量，并通过理论分析保证无偏性和多项式方差增长。

Result: 所提方法在 $m$ 次查询下，仅需 $O\big(p (d + m)\log m\big)$ 随机比特，估计结果无偏，且方差上界为关于 $p$ 的多项式函数，相比已有方法在随机资源消耗和方差稳定性方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 本文提出的新型迹估计器在降低随机复杂度的同时有效控制了方差增长，适用于高维、大规模的迹估计任务，为隐式矩阵迹的高效近似提供了新的理论与实践工具。

Abstract: We consider the problem of estimating the trace of an implicit matrix $\mathbf{A} \in \mathbb{R}^{d^p\times d^p}$ that can only be accessed through matrix-vector products queries. The \textit{Hutchinson trace estimator}% ~\cite{Girard1987algorithme, article-hutchinson} is a classical sketching method for this problem. Their estimator, $H_{m}(\mathbf{A}) = \frac{1}{m} \sum_{i=1}^{m} {\mathbf{z}^{(i)}}^T \mathbf{A} \mathbf{z}^{(i)}, \quad \text{where } \ {\mathbf{z}^{(i)}}\in \mathbb{R}^{d^p}$, and $z^{(i)}_j \in {N}(0, 1), j\in [d^p]$, satisfies the following guarantees: (i) $\mathbb{E}[H_{m}(\mathbf{A})]=\operatorname{tr}(\mathbf{A})$, and (ii) $\mathrm{Var}[H_{m}(\mathbf{A})]=\frac{2}{m}||\mathbf{A}||_F^2$. Generating one query vector $\mathbf{z}^{(i)}$ requires $O(d^p)$ random bits; thus, $m$ queries require $O(md^p)$ random bits, which can be prohibitive in large-scale applications. Recent work by Meyer et al.~\cite{meyer2025hutchinsonsestimatorbadkroneckertraceestimation} proposes a variant of the Hutchinson trace estimator in which each query vector in $\mathbb{R}^{d^p}$ is constructed as the Kronecker product of $p$ random vectors in $\mathbb{R}^d$, requiring $O(mpd)$ random bits for $m$ query vectors. The estimator of~\cite{meyer2025hutchinsonsestimatorbadkroneckertraceestimation} is unbiased; however, its variance grows exponentially with $p$. In this work, we address this limitation by proposing a sketching-based estimator that requires $O\!\big(p (d + m)\log m\big)$ random bits, yields an unbiased estimate of the trace, and simultaneously achieves a variance bound that grows polynomially with $p$.

</details>


### [262] [Label-free steering: Compressing test-time reinforcement learning into bias-only subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18587)
*Naveen Vakada,Mingyuan Li,Shaoxiong Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无标签的仅优化偏置参数的测试时强化学习方法（Label-free bias-only TTRL），仅优化约10万个小规模偏置参数，而冻结预训练主干网络。该方法在MATH-500上达到76.67%准确率，优于自研的有标签偏置引导方法，且参数优化量仅为全参数TTRL的1/76,000。该方法在视觉-语言和音频推理任务中也有效提升性能，并具备跨问题迁移能力。分析表明，多数投票可靠性随推理一致性增强，且具有更高梯度能量可访问性的偏置子空间更具可训练性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时强化学习方法通常需要优化大量模型参数，但实际应用中常面临奖励信号稀缺与计算资源受限的问题。因此，研究在极小优化空间和完全无标签奖励下能否实现有效适应，是关键挑战。

Method: 采用多数投票生成伪标签作为奖励信号，仅对约10万偏置参数进行优化，保持预训练模型主干不变，通过测试时微调实现推理能力提升。

Result: 在MATH-500上取得76.67%准确率，优于有标签基线，参数量减少76,000倍；在多任务（MathVista、AI2D、LogicVista、MMAU）上均有性能提升；所学调整向量可泛化至4,500个未见数学题；分析揭示了多数投票可靠性与推理一致性相关，且高梯度能量偏置子空间更易训练。

Conclusion: 即使在极度受限的奖励信号和极小优化空间条件下，仅优化偏置参数也能实现显著的测试时适应，证明了标签自由、轻量级的偏置调节是一种高效可行的推理增强策略。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) enables models to improve their reasoning without relying on labeled training data, but existing approaches typically optimize a large fraction of the model parameters. This raises a natural question: can effective test-time adaptation emerge when both the reward signal and the optimization space are severely restricted? We answer this question with label-free bias-only TTRL, which uses majority-vote pseudo-labels as rewards and optimizes only approximately 100K bias parameters while keeping the pretrained backbone frozen. On MATH-500, our approach reaches 76.67% accuracy, slightly exceeding our own labeled bias-steering reproduction while optimizing 76,000x fewer parameters than full-parameter TTRL. The same training procedure improves performance across vision-language and audio reasoning tasks, including MathVista, AI2D, LogicVista, and MMAU. We further show that the learned steering vectors transfer to 4,500 held-out MATH problems, indicating that the adaptation is not limited to the problems used during test-time optimization. Finally, we analyze why this highly restricted adaptation can work, showing that majority-vote reliability improves with rollout consensus and that bias subspaces with greater accessible gradient energy exhibit stronger downstream trainability. These results demonstrate that substantial test-time adaptation can emerge from optimizing a tiny bias-only subspace using entirely label-free rewards.

</details>


### [263] [Peak-Aware Short-Term Load Forecasting Across Distribution Grid Aggregation Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18588)
*Souhardya Chattopadhyay,Julian Oelhaf,Antonia Schoening,Jessica Deuschel,Bitan Bhattacharyya,Christian Bergler,Andreas Maier,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在三个配电网络聚合层级（区域代码、二次变电站、低压馈线）上，针对高峰需求时段的短期负荷预测（STLF），比较了统计基线、机器学习模型（LightGBM、XGBoost）和近期时间序列基础模型（Chronos Bolt和Chronos-2）在峰值感知评估框架下的表现。结果表明，Chronos-2在所有层级上均取得最佳高峰时段性能，同时在整体指标上也保持领先或接近最优，相较于梯度提升模型，其平均高峰时段NMAE降低约20%-51%。量化分析揭示了不同聚合层级的运行点特征，且推理速度满足实际部署要求。研究强调了峰值感知评估与层级特定分位数选择对提升配电系统运营相关性的重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有短期负荷预测方法多关注整体精度，忽视高峰需求时段的预测性能，而该时段的误差可能引发拥堵和电压越限风险，影响电网安全运行。因此，亟需发展更贴近运营商实际需求的峰值感知预测方法。

Method: 采用峰值感知评估框架，基于英国和瑞士公开数据集，在区域代码（AC）、二次变电站（SUB）和低压馈线（LV）三个聚合层级上，对比统计模型、机器学习模型（LightGBM、XGBoost）以及时间序列基础模型（Chronos Bolt、Chronos-2）的性能。使用NMAE和MAPE评估整体与高峰时段预测表现，并通过概率输出的分位数分析识别各层级最优运行点，结合运行时间测量评估实用性。

Result: Chronos-2在所有聚合层级上均实现最优高峰时段预测性能，HD-NMAE和HD-MAPE分别为：AC（0.039, 4.53%）、SUB（0.080, 9.45%）、LV（0.138, 16.14%）；Chronos-Bolt次之。相比梯度提升模型，Chronos-2将平均高峰时段NMAE降低20%-51%，同时在整体指标上仍保持最佳或接近最佳。分位数分析揭示了各层级特有的运行点，推理速度满足实时部署需求。

Conclusion: 峰值感知评估与聚合层级特定的分位数选择是提升短期负荷预测在配电系统中运营相关性的有效路径，时间序列基础模型（如Chronos-2）具备良好的性能与实用性，适合应用于实际配电网络管理。

Abstract: For distribution system operators, short-term load forecasting (STLF) supports congestion management, voltage control, and asset protection. Most existing approaches focus on overall accuracy across all time steps and neglect performance during high-demand (HD) periods, where larger forecast errors can increase the risk of congestion and voltage violations. In this paper, we study peak-aware STLF across three operator-relevant distribution grid aggregation levels, area codes (AC), secondary substations (SUB), and low-voltage (LV) feeders, using open datasets from the United Kingdom and Switzerland. We compare statistical baselines, machine learning models (LightGBM and XGBoost), and recent time-series foundation models (Chronos Bolt and Chronos-2) under a peak-aware evaluation framework that reports both overall and HD forecasting performance using NMAE and MAPE. The results show that Chronos-2 achieves the best HD performance across all aggregation levels, with HD-NMAE and HD-MAPE of 0.039 and 4.53% at AC, 0.080 and 9.45% at SUB, and 0.138 and 16.14% at LV, while Chronos-Bolt consistently ranks second best. Compared with the gradient boosted ML models, Chronos-2 reduces mean HD-NMAE by about 20-51% across levels while remaining best or near-best on the overall metrics. A quantile analysis of the probabilistic Chronos outputs further identifies aggregation-specific operating points, and runtime measurements indicate that foundation model inference is fast enough for practical deployment. Overall, the findings highlight peak-aware evaluation and aggregation specific quantile selection as a practical pathway toward more operationally relevant STLF in distribution networks.

</details>


### [264] [ReDIL-GNN: Resynthesis Domain Incremental Learning for Circuit Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18595)
*Rupesh Raj Karn,Johann Knechtel,Ozgur Sinanoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReDIL-GNN框架，用于在电路逻辑重合成导致的领域增量学习场景中，适应新合成风格的同时保持对已有领域的性能。引入了Resynthesis Adaptability Index（RAI）作为预适应评分，综合考虑适应需求、源等价性可恢复性、结构覆盖度和更新兼容性，有效区分应适应与不应适应的领域变化。实验表明RAI能准确评估不同合成流的适应潜力，从0.001到0.824不等，指导模型是否重用、保留或延迟适应，实现可部署的电路学习控制闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电路图神经网络在面对逻辑重合成带来的领域分布变化时，缺乏有效的增量学习机制，难以在不改变任务标签的前提下适应新合成风格，且无法判断何时应进行适应，存在盲目更新风险。

Method: 提出ReDIL-GNN框架，结合领域增量学习策略与自适应筛选机制；设计并使用Resynthesis Adaptability Index（RAI）作为预适应评分，基于适应需要、源等价性可恢复性、结构覆盖度和更新兼容性四个维度评估新合成流的适配价值，并据此决定是否更新模型。

Result: RAI能够有效区分需适应与无需适应的合成变化，其值范围为0.001至0.824，在多种硬件安全任务和表示学习模型上表现优异；相比传统方法（如LwF、Online EWC等），该框架在保留旧域性能的同时更精准地选择适应时机，显著提升实用性与效率。

Conclusion: ReDIL-GNN通过RAI实现了对逻辑重合成引起的领域变化的智能响应，将电路学习过程转化为可部署的控制闭环，使模型能够在动态合成环境中持续高效运行，具备良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Logic resynthesis preserves circuit functionality while changing gate vocabulary, topology, and structural statistics, creating domain shift for circuit graph neural networks (GNNs) without changing task labels. To study this setting, we introduce ReDIL-GNN, a resynthesis domain-incremental learning framework that adapts a fixed prediction or representation head as new synthesis styles arrive and evaluates retention on all previously observed domains. Because not every shift should be adapted blindly, ReDIL-GNN further introduces the Resynthesis Adaptability Index (RAI), a pre-adaptation score that combines adaptation need, source-equivalence recoverability, structural coverage, and update compatibility. We evaluate supervised hardware-security tasks and representation-learning models using task-native metrics for classifiers and source-equivalence retrieval metrics for embedding models, comparing naive fine-tuning with LwF, Online EWC, MAS, ER, A-GEM, DER++, ER+LwF, and equivalence-guided replay. Across the studied pipelines, RAI separates unsupported shifts from promising updates, ranging from 0.001 for a structurally uncovered GNN-RE ABC-rewrite shift to 0.824 for the best original-only GNN-RE adaptation case. In practice, ReDIL-GNN turns resynthesis-aware circuit learning into a deployment control loop: RAI screens each new synthesis flow before update, guiding whether to reuse the current model, apply retention-aware adaptation, or defer adaptation until the shift is better supported.

</details>


### [265] [Online Robust Reinforcement Learning Through Monte-Carlo Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18599)
*Tuan Dam,Kishan Panaganti,Brahim Driss,Adam Wierman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的鲁棒MCTS算法，以应对仿真与现实之间因模型不匹配导致的不确定性。通过引入鲁棒幂均值更新算子和精心设计的探索奖励，该算法在有限样本下保证了搜索树每个节点的收敛性，并实现了根节点价值估计的$\mathcal{O}(n^{-1/2})$收敛速率，与标准MCTS相当。实验表明，该方法在奖励分布和转移动态存在显著不确定性时仍能保持稳健性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中的决策问题常因低保真度模拟器导致建模不匹配，而传统MCTS依赖于仿真与现实一致的假设，难以适应此类不确定性。因此需要一种能够处理动力学和奖励分布模糊性的鲁棒MCTS变体。

Method: 提出一种基于鲁棒幂均值备份操作符的MCTS变体，结合针对状态转移和奖励分布不确定性的探索奖励机制，确保在有限样本下每个节点的收敛性。

Result: 算法在根节点实现$\mathcal{O}(n^{-1/2})$的收敛速率，与标准MCTS相当；在多种存在显著建模误差的规划任务中表现出鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒MCTS算法有效缓解了仿真与现实之间的模型差异问题，提升了复杂决策任务中从仿真到现实部署的可靠性。

Abstract: Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) is a powerful framework for solving complex decision-making problems, yet it often relies on the assumption that the simulator and the real-world dynamics are identical. Although this assumption helps achieve the success of MCTS in games like Chess, Go, and Shogi, the real-world scenarios incur ambiguity due to their modeling mismatches in low-fidelity simulators. In this work, we present a new robust variant of MCTS that mitigates dynamical model ambiguities. Our algorithm addresses transition dynamics and reward distribution ambiguities to bridge the gap between simulation-based planning and real-world deployment. We incorporate a robust power mean backup operator and carefully designed exploration bonuses to ensure finite-sample convergence at every node in the search tree. We show that our algorithm achieves a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(n^{-1/2})$ for the value estimation at the root node, comparable to that of standard MCTS. Finally, we provide empirical evidence that our method achieves robust performance in planning problems even under significant ambiguity in the underlying reward distribution and transition dynamics.

</details>


### [266] [A Geometric Theory of Decision Boundaries in Structured Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18610)
*Fredy Pokou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构化最优策略的几何理论，将策略诱导的决策边界几何作为分析核心，揭示了策略重构、表示复杂度和查询复杂度的本质。在适当的结构正则性条件下，决策边界的几何特性决定了最小表示需求及统计与计算复杂度。研究引入了边界和决策复杂性的内在概念，提出了信息论层面的决策压缩度量，并给出了从黑箱策略查询中进行边界估计和策略重构的统计保证。结果表明，对于所考虑的结构化决策问题，策略重构的复杂度由决策边界的几何而非状态空间的基数决定。数值实验验证了理论预测并提供了支持性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动态规划通过值函数和策略表示最优序列决策，但无法直接揭示策略重构、表示复杂度或查询复杂度的数学本质。本文旨在填补这一空白，明确决策边界几何在策略重构中的核心作用。

Method: 构建结构化最优策略的几何理论，以决策边界几何为分析主对象；在结构正则性假设下，分析其对策略重构最小表示、统计与计算复杂度的影响；引入内在的边界与决策复杂度概念，建立信息论度量，并推导基于黑箱查询的边界估计与策略重构的统计保证。
